  链接: https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/actions/runs/39562178793/job/119096436626

- **base-b-test-1-npu-a3 / run (0)**: 在 base-b-test-1-npu-a3 作业中，test/registered/npu/basic_function/speculative_inference/test_npu_eagle3.py 以退出码 5 失败（pytest 中通常表示未收集到测试或测试收集错误），导致整个作业退出码为 1。其余 5 个 NPU 基础功能测试均通过。
  - 失败用例: npu_eagle3.py
  链接: https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/actions/runs/39562178793/job/119096436640

- **base-b-test-2-npu-a3 / run (0)**: 日志显示模型初始化正常，随后报错“Executing the custom container implementation failed”，属于 runner 容器环境异常，非代码或精度问题，需联系 runner 管理员排查容器运行时。
  链接: https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/actions/runs/39562178793/job/119096436660

- **multimodal-gen-test-4-npu-a3 (1)**: 日志显示“No files were found with the provided path: diffusion-failures/. No artifacts will be uploaded.”，说明测试未生成预期失败产物目录，upload-artifact 步骤无文件可上传而失败；其余仅为 Node 弃用警告，非根因。
  链接: https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/actions/runs/39562178793/job/119096436793
